# ---- 06_morocco_lands_of_anarchy.txt ----
REPLACE:je_lands_of_anarchy = {
	group = je_group_historical_content
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

    is_shown_in_lobby = {
        c:MOR ?= this
        has_dlc_feature = ip4_content
    }

	scripted_progress_bar = lands_of_anarchy_tribes_of_marrakech_bar
	scripted_progress_bar = lands_of_anarchy_tribes_of_al_rif_bar
	scripted_progress_bar = lands_of_anarchy_tribes_of_inner_morocco_bar
	scripted_button = je_lands_of_anarchy_harka_activate_button
	scripted_button = je_lands_of_anarchy_harka_deactivate_button
	scripted_button = je_lands_of_anarchy_colonize_sahara_button


		
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
	transferable = no
	weight = 10000

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { name = al_rif_progress_bar_current_var value = 260 }
		set_variable = { name = inner_morocco_progress_bar_current_var value = 260 }
		set_variable = { name = marrakech_progress_bar_current_var value = 260 }
		trigger_event = lands_of_anarchy.1
		s:STATE_TUNISIA = {
			save_scope_as = al_rif_scope
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = root
				}
				save_scope_as = al_rif_state_scope
				add_modifier = {
					name = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
					multiplier = {
						value = var:al_rif_progress_bar_current_var
						divide = -100
						add = 5.15
					}
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_TUNISIA = {
			save_scope_as = marrakech_scope
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = root
				}
				save_scope_as = marrakech_state_scope
				add_modifier = {
					name = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
					multiplier = {
						value = var:marrakech_progress_bar_current_var
						divide = -100
						add = 5.15
					}
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_TUNISIA = {
			save_scope_as = inner_morocco_scope
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = root
				}
				save_scope_as = inner_morocco_state_scope
				add_modifier = {
					name = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
					multiplier = {
						value = var:inner_morocco_progress_bar_current_var
						divide = -100
						add = 5.15
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	modifiers_while_active = {
		lands_of_anarchy_incorporation_mod
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		random_events = {
			350 = 0
			5 = lands_of_anarchy.4 #tribal gesture
			5 = lands_of_anarchy.5 #tribal gesture
		}
		effect = {
            set_variable = { name = al_rif_progress_bar_current_var value = "scope:journal_entry.scripted_bar_progress(lands_of_anarchy_tribes_of_al_rif_bar)" }
            scope:al_rif_state_scope ?= {
            	if = {
            		limit = { owner = root }
            		remove_modifier = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
            		add_modifier = {
            			name = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
            			multiplier = {
            				value = var:al_rif_progress_bar_current_var
            				divide = -100
            				add = 5.15
            			}
            		}
            	}
            	else = {
            		remove_modifier = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
            	}
            }
            set_variable = { name = marrakech_progress_bar_current_var value = "scope:journal_entry.scripted_bar_progress(lands_of_anarchy_tribes_of_marrakech_bar)" }
            scope:marrakech_state_scope ?= {
            	if = {
            		limit = { owner = root }
            		remove_modifier = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
            		add_modifier = {
            			name = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
            			multiplier = {
            				value = var:marrakech_progress_bar_current_var
            				divide = -100
            				add = 5.15
            			}
            		}
            	}
            	else = {
            		remove_modifier = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
            	}
            }

            set_variable = { name = inner_morocco_progress_bar_current_var value = "scope:journal_entry.scripted_bar_progress(lands_of_anarchy_tribes_of_inner_morocco_bar)" }
            scope:inner_morocco_state_scope ?= {
            	if = {
            		limit = { owner = root }
            		remove_modifier = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
            		add_modifier = {
            			name = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
            			multiplier = {
            				value = var:inner_morocco_progress_bar_current_var
            				divide = -100
            				add = 5.15
            			}
            		}
            	}
            	else = {
            		remove_modifier = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
            	}
            }
		}
	}

	complete = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = je_lands_of_anarchy_complete_tt
			AND = {
				je:je_lands_of_anarchy = {
					"scripted_bar_progress(lands_of_anarchy_tribes_of_marrakech_bar)" >= 400
				}
				je:je_lands_of_anarchy = {
					"scripted_bar_progress(lands_of_anarchy_tribes_of_al_rif_bar)" >= 400
				}
				je:je_lands_of_anarchy = {
					"scripted_bar_progress(lands_of_anarchy_tribes_of_inner_morocco_bar)" >= 400
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_complete = {
		s:STATE_TUNISIA = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_state = {
						NOT = {
							owner = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				add_claim = ROOT
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_mod
				}
				remove_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_mod
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					ruler ?= {
						has_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_popularity_mod
					}
				}
				ruler ?= {
					remove_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_popularity_mod
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_event = lands_of_anarchy.2
	}

	fail = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = je_lands_of_anarchy_fail_tt
			AND = {
				je:je_lands_of_anarchy = {
					"scripted_bar_progress(lands_of_anarchy_tribes_of_marrakech_bar)" <= 100
				}
				je:je_lands_of_anarchy = {
					"scripted_bar_progress(lands_of_anarchy_tribes_of_al_rif_bar)" <= 100
				}
				je:je_lands_of_anarchy = {
					"scripted_bar_progress(lands_of_anarchy_tribes_of_inner_morocco_bar)"<= 100
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_fail = {
		trigger_event = lands_of_anarchy.3
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_mod
				}
				remove_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_mod
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					ruler ?= {
						has_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_popularity_mod
					}
				}
				ruler ?= {
					remove_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_popularity_mod
				}
			}
		}
	}

	invalid = {
		any_scope_state = {
			has_modifier = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
			count < 2
		}
		hidden_trigger = {
			has_variable = anarchy_modifiers_given_var
		}
	}

	on_invalid = {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_modifier = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = lands_of_anarchy_failed_nation_mod
			days = long_modifier_time
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_mod
				}
				remove_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_mod
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					ruler ?= {
						has_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_popularity_mod
					}
				}
				ruler ?= {
					remove_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_popularity_mod
				}
			}
		}
	}

	timeout = 9125

	on_timeout = {
		add_modifier = {
			name = lands_of_anarchy_failed_nation_mod
			days = long_modifier_time
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					remove_modifier = state_of_anarchy_scaled_mod
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_mod
				}
				remove_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_mod
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					ruler ?= {
						has_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_popularity_mod
					}
				}
				ruler ?= {
					remove_modifier = lands_of_anarchy_harka_popularity_mod
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

        random_scope_state = {
            limit = {
                state_region = s:STATE_LUZON
            }
            save_scope_as = visayas_scope
        }
        random_scope_state = {
            limit = {
                state_region = s:STATE_LUZON
            }
            save_scope_as = mindanao_scope

        gdp >= 2000000

                type = bg_plantations

            }

                    is_homeland = cu:filipino_mestizo
                }
                any_scope_building = {
                    is_building_type = building_urban_center
                    level >= 5
                }
            }
        }
        s:STATE_LUZON = {
            any_scope_state = {
                owner = root
                is_incorporated = yes
                has_building = building_port
            }
        }
        s:STATE_LUZON = {
            any_scope_state = {
                owner = root
                is_incorporated = yes
                has_building = building_port
            }
        }
    }

    on_complete = {
        trigger_event = { id = philippines.10 }
        hidden_effect = {
            set_variable = phi_dev_done
        }
    }

    event_outcome_completed_effect_desc = {
        header = philippines10a_tt

            s:STATE_LUZON = {

                pop_type = aristocrats

                value = medium_radicals
                pop_type = capitalists
            }

                name = modifier_growth_of_the_middle_class
                days = long_modifier_time
            }
            custom_tooltip = {
                text = unlocks_je_philippines_main_ilustrado_button_tt
            }
        }
    }

    event_outcome_completed_effect_desc = {
        header = philippines10b_tt
        effect = {
            s:STATE_LUZON = {
                add_state_trait = state_trait_port_of_davao
            }
            add_loyalists = {
                value = medium_radicals
                pop_type = aristocrats
            }
            add_loyalists = {
                value = medium_radicals
                pop_type = capitalists
            }
            add_modifier = {
                name = modifier_growth_of_the_middle_class
                days = long_modifier_time
            }
            custom_tooltip = {
                text = unlocks_je_philippines_main_ilustrado_button_tt
            }
        }
    }

    fail = {
        OR = {
            NOT = { 
                has_state_in_state_region = STATE_LUZON
            }
            NOT = { 
                has_state_in_state_region = STATE_LUZON
            }
        }
    }

    on_fail = {
        add_modifier = {
            name = modifier_developmental_dearth
            days = normal_modifier_time
        }
        add_modifier = {
            name = modifier_archipelagic_anger
            days = normal_modifier_time
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_modifier = philippines_development_modifier_a
            }
            remove_modifier = philippines_development_modifier_a
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_modifier = philippines_development_modifier_b
            }
            remove_modifier = philippines_development_modifier_b
        }
    }


    invalid = {
        NOT = {
            c:PHI ?= this
        }
    }

    weight = 1000
    transferable = no
    	
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
}

            }

    fail = {
        custom_tooltip = {
            text = no_insurrectionary_movement_tt
            NOT = {
                any_political_movement = {
                    is_insurrectionary = yes
                }
            }
        }
        AND = {
            OR = {
                is_subject_type = subject_type_dominion
                is_subject_type = subject_type_protectorate
                is_subject_type = subject_type_personal_union
            }
            custom_tooltip = {
                text = spain_is_overlord_tt
                top_overlord = {
                    c:SPA ?= this
                }
            }
            c:SPA ?= {
                relations:root >= relations_threshold:amicable
            }
        }
        legitimacy >= 50
        liberty_desire <= 25
    }

    on_fail = {
        trigger_event = { id = philippines.3 popup = yes }
        hidden_effect = {
            set_variable = je_philippines_main_reliance_happened
        }
    }
    custom_on_failure_header = je_philippines_main_header_on_reliance_complete

	weight = 5000

    	
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
}

REPLACE:je_conquest_of_tetouan = { #al rif over border disputes
	group = je_group_historical_content
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

		
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
	weight = 5000

	is_shown_when_inactive = {
		has_technology_researched = colonization
		OR = {
			c:SPA ?= this
			c:IBE ?= this
		}
	}

	modifiers_while_active = {
		modifier_african_adventure
	}

	possible = {
		has_technology_researched = nationalism
		s:STATE_TUNISIA = {
			AND = {
				any_scope_state = {
					OR = {
						owner ?= ROOT
						owner = {
							is_subject_of = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				any_scope_state = {
					owner ?= {
						relations:root <= relations_threshold:poor
						NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
						OR = {
							c:MOR ?= this
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:maghrebi
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:berber
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		trigger_event = spanish_africa.1
		s:STATE_TUNISIA = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:MOR ?= this
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:maghrebi
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:berber
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = al_rif
				owner = {
					save_scope_as = rif_enemy_scope
				}
			}
		}
		c:MOR ?= {
			save_scope_as = morocco_scope
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces = {
			save_scope_as = armed_forces_scope
		}
	}

	complete = {
		country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region  = STATE_TUNISIA
	}

	on_complete = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_technology_researched = civilizing_mission
				NOT = {
					has_law = law_type:law_no_colonial_affairs
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_TUNISIA
					}
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = spanish_africa_2_firing_from_je
					days = 90
				}
				trigger_event = spanish_africa.2
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						any_state_in_subsaharan_africa = {
							custom_tooltip = {
								text = owned_by_root_or_a_colonial_subject_tt
								owner = {
									OR = {
										this = ROOT
										AND = {
											country_is_colonial_or_company = yes
											is_subject_of = ROOT
										}
									}
								}
							}
							count >= 3
						}
					}
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = spanish_africa_3_firing_from_je
					days = 90
				}
				trigger_event = spanish_africa.3
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = {
							c:MOR ?= {
								is_subject_of = ROOT
							}
						}
						NOT = {
							AND = {
								country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region  = STATE_TUNISIA
								country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region  = STATE_TUNISIA
								country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region  = STATE_TUNISIA
								country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region  = STATE_TUNISIA
							}
						}
					}
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = spanish_africa_4_firing_from_je
					days = 90
				}
				trigger_event = spanish_africa.4
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = successful_retaliation_modifier
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
				trigger_event = spanish_africa.9
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = further_events_upon_researching_civilising_mission_tt
		}
	}

	timeout = 3650

	on_timeout = {
		trigger_event = spanish_africa.10
		s:STATE_TUNISIA = {
			remove_claim = root
		}
	}
}

REPLACE:je_the_first_carlist_war = {
	group = je_group_global_international_situations
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_carlist_beret.dds"

    is_shown_in_lobby = {
        OR = {
        	c:SPA ?= this
        	c:SPC ?= this
        }
    }

	modifiers_while_active = {
		carlist_war_guerilla_warfare
	}

	scripted_progress_bar = je_the_first_carlist_war_score_bar
	scripted_button = je_the_first_carlist_war_royal_expedition_button
	scripted_button = je_the_first_carlist_flush_them_out_button

	should_be_involved = {
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:spanish
		is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_iberia_old
	}

	should_show_when_not_involved = {
		always = no
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_variable = spa_carlist_war_score }
			}
			set_global_variable = {
				name = spa_carlist_war_score
				value = 0
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_variable = spc_carlist_war_score }
			}
			set_global_variable = {
				name = spc_carlist_war_score
				value = 0
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_variable = carlist_war_skirmish_phase }
			}
			set_global_variable = carlist_war_skirmish_phase
		}
	}

	immediate_all_involved = {
		set_variable = coup_immunity
	}

	status_desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = je_the_first_carlist_war_status
				trigger = {
					has_global_variable = carlist_war_skirmish_phase
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = je_the_first_carlist_war_status_royal_expedition
				trigger = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			c:SPC ?= {
				random_list = {
					9 = {}
					1 = { trigger_event = carlist_war.5 }
					1 = { trigger_event = carlist_war.6 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	complete = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = je_the_first_carlist_war_complete
			NAND = {
				exists = c:SPC
				exists = c:SPA
			}
		}
	}

	on_complete_all_involved = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:SPC ?= this
			}
			change_tag = SPA
			set_capital = STATE_CATALONIA
			set_market_capital = STATE_CATALONIA
			set_variable = spain_carlist_victory
			add_modifier = {
				name = carlist_war_spc_reward
				years = 20
				is_decaying = yes
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						state_region = {
							is_homeland = cu:spanish
						}
					}
					set_state_type = incorporated
				}
			}
			set_variable = is_carlist_spain
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				c:SPA ?= this
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = carlist_war_spa_reward
				years = 20
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		trigger_event = carlist_war.2
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_global_variable = spa_carlist_war_score
			remove_global_variable = spc_carlist_war_score

			remove_modifier = the_last_stand

			remove_modifier = spc_secure_port

			remove_modifier = captured_royal_arsenals

			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_state = {
						has_modifier = spa_carlist_terror
					}
				}
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						has_modifier = spa_carlist_terror
					}
					remove_modifier = spa_carlist_terror
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_State = {
						has_modifier = ad_hoc_manufacturing
					}
				}
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						has_modifier = ad_hoc_manufacturing
					}
					remove_modifier = ad_hoc_manufacturing
				}
			}
			if = {

					any_scope_state = {

					}
				}
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {

					}
					remove_modifier = spc_raided_grain_storages

REPLACE:je_taming_the_north = {
    icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"

    group = je_group_historical_content

    scripted_button = button_je_taming_the_north_establish_colonies
    scripted_button = button_je_taming_the_north_integrate_ainu
    scripted_button = button_je_taming_the_north_sapporo_agricultural_college
    scripted_button = button_je_taming_the_north_agriculture_potentials
    scripted_button = button_je_taming_the_north_agriculture_arable_land

    is_shown_when_inactive = {
        has_dlc_feature = ep2_content
        OR = {
            c:JAP ?= this
            AND = {
                c:EZO ?= this
                OR = {
                    has_government_type = gov_ezo_republic
                    has_government_type = gov_ezo_republic_colonial
                }
            }
        }
        s:STATE_KANTO = {
            any_scope_state = {
                owner = root
            }
        }
    }

    possible = {
        owns_entire_state_region = STATE_KANTO
        trigger_if = {
            limit = {
                c:JAP ?= ROOT
            }
            s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:JAP ?= {
                is_incorporated = yes
                any_scope_building = {
                    is_building_type = building_urban_center
                    level >= 1
                }
            }
        }
        trigger_else_if = {
            limit = {
                c:EZO ?= ROOT
            }
            s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:EZO ?= {
                is_incorporated = yes
                any_scope_building = {
                    is_building_type = building_urban_center
                    level >= 1
                }
            }
        }
    }

    immediate = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    c:JAP ?= ROOT
                    is_subject_of = c:JAP
                }
            }
            c:JAP ?= {
                save_scope_as = japan_scope # Intentionally invalidating this scope if the Ezo Republic is independent
            }
        }
        trigger_event = {
            id = hokkaido_events.1
            popup = yes
        }
        s:STATE_KANTO = {
            random_scope_state = {
                limit = {
                    owner = root
                }
                save_scope_as = hokkaido_state_scope
            }
        }
        s:STATE_KANTO = {
            save_scope_as = sakhalin_state_region_scope
        }
        set_variable = {
            name = hokkaido_agriculture_potentials_counter_var
            value = 0
        }
        set_variable = {
            name = hokkaido_agriculture_arable_counter_var
            value = 0
        }
        set_variable = {
            name = ainu_friendship_var
            value = 0
        }
    }

    on_monthly_pulse = {
    }

    on_yearly_pulse = {
        random_events = {
        }
    }

    complete = {
        trigger_if = {
            limit = {
                c:JAP ?= ROOT
            }
            s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:JAP ?= {
                state_population > 500000
                gdp > 1000000
            }
        }
        trigger_else_if = {
            limit = {
                c:EZO ?= ROOT
            }
            s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:EZO ?= {
                state_population > 500000
                gdp > 1000000
            }
        }
        custom_tooltip = {
            text = taming_the_north_agriculture_potentials_goal_tt
            exists = var:hokkaido_agriculture_potentials_counter_var
            var:hokkaido_agriculture_potentials_counter_var >= 3
        }
        custom_tooltip = {
            text = taming_the_north_agriculture_arable_goal_tt
            exists = var:hokkaido_agriculture_arable_counter_var
            var:hokkaido_agriculture_arable_counter_var >= 3
        }
        custom_tooltip = {
            text = taming_the_north_colonies_goal_tt
            has_variable = button_je_taming_the_north_establish_colonies_var
        }
        
    }

    on_complete = {
        trigger_event = {
            id = hokkaido_events.7
            popup = yes
        }
        show_as_tooltip = {
            add_modifier = {
                name = modifier_northern_learnings
                days = very_long_modifier_time
            }
            scope:hokkaido_state_scope ?= {
                add_modifier = {
                    name = modifier_tamed_north
                    days = very_long_modifier_time
                }
            }
            if = {
                limit = {
                    NOT = {
                        has_journal_entry = je_taming_the_north
                    }
                }
                custom_tooltip = {
                    text = je_taming_the_north_additional_goal_pre
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = {
                    NOR = {
                        owns_entire_state_region = STATE_KANTO
                        overlord ?= {
                            owns_entire_state_region = STATE_KANTO
                        }
                    }
                }
                custom_tooltip = {
                    text = je_taming_the_north_additional_goal
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        owns_entire_state_region = STATE_KANTO
                        overlord ?= {
                            owns_entire_state_region = STATE_KANTO
                        }
                    }
                }
                custom_tooltip = je_taming_the_north_additional_goal_completed
                s:STATE_KANTO = {
                    every_scope_state = {
                        limit = {
                            owner = {
                                ROOT ?= this
                                overlord ?= this
                            }
                        }
                        add_modifier = {
                            name = modifier_tamed_north
                            days = very_long_modifier_time
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        remove_variable = hokkaido_agriculture_potentials_counter_var
        remove_variable = hokkaido_agriculture_arable_counter_var
        remove_variable = button_je_taming_the_north_establish_colonies_var
    }

    event_outcome_completed_effect_desc = {
        header = hokkaido_events.7.a
        effect = {
            add_modifier = {
                name = modifier_external_applications
                days = long_modifier_time
            }
        }
    }

    event_outcome_completed_effect_desc = {
        header = hokkaido_events.7.b
        effect = {
            add_modifier = {
                name = modifier_domestic_applications
                days = long_modifier_time
            }
        }
    }

    fail = {
        NOT = {
            has_state_in_state_region = STATE_KANTO
        }
    }

    on_fail = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                c:JAP ?= ROOT
            }
            trigger_event = {
                id = hokkaido_events.8
                popup = yes
            }
        }
    }

    event_outcome_failed_effect_desc = {
        header = hokkaido_events.8.a
        effect = {
            if = {
                limit = {
                    c:JAP ?= ROOT
                }
                add_radicals = {
                    value = medium_radicals
                    culture = cu:japanese
                }
                ig:ig_industrialists = {
                    add_modifier = {
                        name = modifier_lost_northern_investments
                        days = normal_modifier_time
                    }
                }
                ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie = {
                    add_modifier = {
                        name = modifier_lost_northern_investments
                        days = normal_modifier_time
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    invalid = {
    }

    weight = 5000

      
    should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
}


# ---- 07_japanese_religion.txt ----
REPLACE:je_shinbutsu_bunri = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/religion_icons/shinto.dds"

	group = je_group_historical_content

	possible = {
		# triggered by shinbutsu_bunri_decision
	}

	scripted_button = je_taikyo_proclamation_button

	immediate = {
		every_scope_homeland_state = {
			convert_population = {
				target = rel:shinto
				value = 0.10
			}
		}
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = { is_homeland = cu:japanese }
					religion_percent_state = {  
						target = rel:shinto
						value <= 0.5
					}
				}
				convert_population = {
					target = rel:shinto
					value = 0.02
				}
			}
		}
		random_events = {
			100 = 0
			10 = japan_religion.1
			10 = japan_religion.2
			10 = japan_religion.3
			10 = japan_religion.4
			10 = japan_religion.5
			10 = japan_religion.6
		}
	}

	complete = {
		# All starting Japanese Homelands
		# Trigger targets states individually for clearer tooltip
		s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:JAP ?= {
			religion_percent_state = {
				target = rel:shinto
				value >= 0.6
			}
		}
		s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:JAP ?= {
			religion_percent_state = {
				target = rel:shinto
				value >= 0.6
			}
		}
		s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:JAP ?= {
			religion_percent_state = {
				target = rel:shinto
				value >= 0.6
			}
		}
		s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:JAP ?= {
			religion_percent_state = {
				target = rel:shinto
				value >= 0.6
			}
		}
		s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:JAP ?= {
			religion_percent_state = {
				target = rel:shinto
				value >= 0.6
			}
		}
		s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:JAP ?= {
			religion_percent_state = {
				target = rel:shinto
				value >= 0.6
			}
		}
		s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:JAP ?= {
			religion_percent_state = {
				target = rel:shinto
				value >= 0.6
			}
		}
		s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:JAP ?= {
			religion_percent_state = {
				target = rel:shinto
				value >= 0.6
			}
		}
		s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:JAP ?= {
			religion_percent_state = {
				target = rel:shinto
				value >= 0.6
			}
		}
		s:STATE_KANTO.region_state:JAP ?= {
			religion_percent_state = {
				target = rel:shinto
				value >= 0.6
			}
		}
	}

	on_complete = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = japan_religion.11
			popup = yes
		}
	}

	event_outcome_completed_effect_desc = {
		effect = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = arahitogami
			}
			every_scope_homeland_state = {
				convert_population = {
					target = rel:shinto
					value = 0.1
				}
			}
		}
	}

	fail = {
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_total_separation
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_state_atheism
			AND = {
				NOT = { country_has_state_religion = rel:shinto }
				NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_freedom_of_conscience }
			}
		}
	}

	invalid = {
	}

	weight = 5000

	timeout = 9125

	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
}

REPLACE:je_elevate_buddhism = {
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/religion_icons/mahayana.dds"

	group = je_group_historical_content

	possible = {
		# triggered by elevate_buddhism_decision
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			name = je_elevate_buddhism_var
			value = 0
		}
	}

	on_monthly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					ig:ig_devout = { is_powerful = yes }
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = je_elevate_buddhism_var
					add = 1
				}
			}
		}
		random_events = {
			100 = 0
			10 = japan_religion.7
			10 = japan_religion.8
			10 = japan_religion.9
			10 = japan_religion.6
		}
	}

	complete = {
		scope:journal_entry = {
			is_goal_complete = yes
		}
		ig:ig_devout = { is_powerful = yes }
	}

	on_complete = {
		trigger_event = {
			id = japan_religion.12
			popup = yes
		}
	}

	event_outcome_completed_effect_desc = {
		effect = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = extended_danka_system
			}
		}
	}

	fail = {
		OR = {
			ig:ig_devout = { is_marginal = yes }
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_total_separation
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_state_atheism
		}
	}

	invalid = {
	}

	current_value = {
		value = root.var:je_elevate_buddhism_var
	}

	goal_add_value = {
		add = 60
	}

	progressbar = yes

	weight = 5000

	timeout = 7300

	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
}



# ---- 07_korea_colonization.txt ----
REPLACE:je_colonize_korea = {
	should_be_pinned_by_default_uninvolved_or_context = yes
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"

	group = je_group_historical_content

	modifiers_while_active = {

	}

	is_shown_when_inactive = {
		has_dlc_feature = ep2_content
		c:JAP ?= this
		NOT = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_isolationism
		}
	}

	possible = {
		institution_investment_level = {
			institution = institution_colonial_affairs
			value >= 1
		}
		NOT = { has_law = law_type:law_no_colonial_affairs }
		AND = {
			owns_entire_state_region = STATE_SEOUL
			owns_entire_state_region = STATE_SEOUL
			owns_entire_state_region = STATE_SEOUL
			owns_entire_state_region = STATE_SEOUL
			owns_entire_state_region = STATE_SEOUL
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		every_state_in_korea = {
			root = {
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = colonize_korea_states_states
					target = prev
				}
			}
		}

		cu:korean = {
			save_scope_as = korean_scope
		}
		cu:japanese = {
			save_scope_as = japanese_scope
		}
 		s:STATE_SEOUL ?= {
 			save_scope_as = seoul_state_scope
 		}
		s:STATE_SEOUL ?= {
			save_scope_as = busan_state_scope
		}
 		s:STATE_SEOUL ?= {
 			save_scope_as = yangho_state_scope
 		}
 		s:STATE_SEOUL ?= {
 			save_scope_as = sariwon_state_scope
		}
 		s:STATE_SEOUL ?= {
 			save_scope_as = pyongyang_state_scope
		}

		add_fervor_target_modifier = { # changes target
			culture = cu:korean
			days = normal_modifier_time
			multiplier = 5
		}
	}

	on_weekly_pulse = {
		effect = {
			clear_variable_list = colonize_korea_states_states
			every_state_in_korea = {
				limit = {
					NAND = {
						c:JAP ?= owner
						is_incorporated = yes
						turmoil < je_colonize_korea_state_turmoil_max
						culture_percent_state = {
							target = cu:japanese
							value >= je_colonize_korea_state_japanese_culture_min # set in event_values
						}
					}
				}
				root = {
					add_to_variable_list = {
						name = colonize_korea_states_states
						target = prev
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# Japan owns Korea directly
	complete = {
		institution_investment_level = {
			institution = institution_colonial_affairs
			value >= 3
		}
		bureaucracy >= 0
		approaching_bureaucracy_shortage = no
		OR = {
			any_company = {
				OR = {
					is_company_type = company_type:company_mantetsu
					is_company_type = company_type:company_oriental_development_company
				}
				company_prosperity >= 75
			}
			AND = { # second option to account for je_zaibatsu
				any_company = {
	                company_is_prosperous = yes
	                count >= 2
	            }
				any_scope_state = {
					is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_korea
					count >= 3
	                OR = {
	                    state_has_building_group_levels = {
	                        type = bg_plantations
	                        value >= 35
	                    }
	                    state_has_building_group_levels = {
	                        type = bg_mining
	                        value >= 35
	                    }
	                    state_has_building_type_levels = {
	                        target = bt:building_railway
	                        value >= 10
	                    }
	                }
				}
			}
		}
		any_state_in_korea = {
			count = all
			custom_tooltip = {
                text = "JE_COLONIZE_KOREA_STATES_TT"
				c:JAP ?= owner
				is_incorporated = yes
				turmoil < je_colonize_korea_state_turmoil_max
				culture_percent_state = {
					target = cu:japanese
					value >= je_colonize_korea_state_japanese_culture_min # set in event_values
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_complete = {
		clear_variable_list = colonize_korea_states_states
		trigger_event = {
			id = korea_colonization.2
			days = 1
		}
	}

	event_outcome_completed_effect_desc = {
		header = korea_colonization.2.a
        effect = {
	    	add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_peninsula_control_prestige_authority
				days = moderately_long_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						state_region = s:STATE_SEOUL
						state_region = s:STATE_SEOUL
						state_region = s:STATE_SEOUL
						state_region = s:STATE_SEOUL
						state_region = s:STATE_SEOUL
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_peninsula_control_assimilation_migration
					days = moderately_long_modifier_time
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
        }
	}

	event_outcome_completed_effect_desc = {
		header = korea_colonization.2.b
		effect = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = korea_colinzation_2b_executive_tt
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = korea_colinzation_2b_company_tt
			}
		}
	}

	# Korea broke free from Japan control
	fail = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = je_colonize_korea_fail_tt
			OR = {
				AND = {
					NOT = {
						any_scope_state = {
							is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_korea
							count >= 5
						}
					}
					c:KOR ?= {
						NOT = {
							is_subject_of = root
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					exists = c:KOR
					c:KOR ?= {
						NOT = {
							is_subject_of = root
						}
					}
				}
				any_state_in_korea = {
					count = all
					NOT = {
						owner = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	on_fail = {
		clear_variable_list = colonize_korea_states_states
		trigger_event = {
			id = korea_colonization.3
			days = 1
		}
	}

	event_outcome_failed_effect_desc = {
        header = korea_colonization.3.a
        effect = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_peninsula_control_prestige_loss
				days = moderately_long_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
        }
    }

	event_outcome_failed_effect_desc = {
		header = korea_colinzation_3b_tt
        effect = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = korea_colinzation_3b_ig_leader_tt
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = korea_colinzation_3b_ig_tt
			}
        }
    }

    invalid = {
    	has_law = law_type:law_no_colonial_affairs
    }

	on_invalid = {
		clear_variable_list = colonize_korea_states_states
	}

	weight = 1000
}
